===== M0003 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 452-481
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
452 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
453 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
454 |     int type = n.getType();
455 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
456 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
457 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
458 |     }
459 | 
460 |     curNode = n;
461 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
462 | 
463 |     switch (type) {
464 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
465 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
466 |         break;
467 | 
468 |       default:
469 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
470 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
471 |           // would no longer point to the true next
472 |           Node next = child.getNext();
473 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
474 |           child = next;
475 |         }
476 |         break;
477 |     }
478 | 
479 |     curNode = n;
480 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
481 |   }

===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 151-196
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
151 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
152 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
153 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
154 |       case Token.BLOCK:
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |       case Token.TRY:
157 |       case Token.FINALLY:
158 |         return true;
159 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
160 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
161 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
162 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
163 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
164 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
165 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
166 |         // child.
167 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
168 |       case Token.WHILE:
169 |       case Token.DO:
170 |       case Token.IF:
171 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
172 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
173 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.FOR:
176 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
177 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
178 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
179 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
180 |         // That way the following:
181 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
182 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
183 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
184 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
185 |         } else {
186 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
187 |         }
188 |       case Token.SWITCH:
189 |       case Token.CASE:
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |       case Token.WITH:
192 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
193 |       default:
194 |         return false;
195 |     }
196 |   }

Method code:
118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:
125 |       case Token.COMMA:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {
206 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:
241 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
242 |       case Token.NAME:
243 |         return processName((Name) node);
244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.CONST:
265 |       case Token.VAR:
266 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
267 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
268 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
269 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
270 |         } else {
271 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
272 |                                           node.getClass() +
273 |                                           " type: " +
274 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
275 |         }
276 |       case Token.WHILE:
277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:
279 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
280 |     }
281 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
282 |   }

===== M0022 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.LinkedDirectedGraph.isConnectedInDirection(N,Predicate<E>,N)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/LinkedDirectedGraph.java
Original range: 203-215
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
208 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
209 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {
210 |         return true;
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     return false;
215 |   }

===== M0024 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 267-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Performs a single flow through a node.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if the flow state differs from the previous state.
   */
Method code:
267 |   protected boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
268 |     FlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
269 |     if (isForward()) {
270 |       L outBefore = state.out;
271 |       state.out = flowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
272 |       return !outBefore.equals(state.out);
273 |     } else {
274 |       L inBefore = state.in;
275 |       state.in = flowThrough(node.getValue(), state.out);
276 |       return !inBefore.equals(state.in);
277 |     }
278 |   }

===== M0030 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateFunctions(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 234-256
Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
240 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
241 |            arg != null;
242 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
243 |         String argName = arg.getString();
244 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }
255 |       }
256 |     }

/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
903 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
904 |     // unknown
905 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
906 |       return true;
907 |     }
908 |     // equality
909 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
910 |       return true;
911 |     }
912 |     // all type
913 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
914 |       return true;
915 |     }
916 |     // unions
917 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
918 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
919 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
920 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
921 |           return true;
922 |         }
923 |       }
924 |     }
925 |     // named types
926 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
927 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
928 |     }
929 |     return false;
930 |   }

===== M0040 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 459-474
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
459 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
460 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
461 |       Set<String> directives = null;
462 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
463 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
464 |         if (directives == null) {
465 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
466 |         } else {
467 |           directives.add(directive);
468 |         }
469 |       }
470 | 
471 |       if (directives != null) {
472 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
473 |       }
474 |     }

===== M0045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleFor(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 395-430
Documentation:

Method code:
395 |   private void handleFor(Node forNode) {
396 |     if (forNode.getChildCount() == 4) {
397 |       // We have for (init; cond; iter) { body }
398 |       Node init = forNode.getFirstChild();
399 |       Node cond = init.getNext();
400 |       Node iter = cond.getNext();
401 |       Node body = iter.getNext();
402 |       // After initialization, we transfer to the FOR which is in charge of
403 |       // checking the condition (for the first time).
404 |       createEdge(init, Branch.UNCOND, forNode);
405 |       // The edge that transfer control to the beginning of the loop body.
406 |       createEdge(forNode, Branch.ON_TRUE, computeFallThrough(body));
407 |       // The edge to end of the loop.
408 |       createEdge(forNode, Branch.ON_FALSE,
409 |           computeFollowNode(forNode, this));
410 |       // The end of the body will have a unconditional branch to our iter
411 |       // (handled by calling computeFollowNode of the last instruction of the
412 |       // body. Our iter will jump to the forNode again to another condition
413 |       // check.
414 |       createEdge(iter, Branch.UNCOND, forNode);
415 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(init, init);
416 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(forNode, cond);
417 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(iter, iter);
418 |     } else {
419 |       // We have for (item in collection) { body }
420 |       Node item = forNode.getFirstChild();
421 |       Node collection = item.getNext();
422 |       Node body = collection.getNext();
423 |       // The edge that transfer control to the beginning of the loop body.
424 |       createEdge(forNode, Branch.ON_TRUE, computeFallThrough(body));
425 |       // The edge to end of the loop.
426 |       createEdge(forNode, Branch.ON_FALSE,
427 |           computeFollowNode(forNode, this));
428 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(forNode, collection);
429 |     }
430 |   }

1013 |      * Returns a node comparator based on the pre-order traversal of the AST.
1014 |      * @param isForward x 'before' y in the pre-order traversal implies
1015 |      * x 'less than' y (if true) and x 'greater than' y (if false).

===== M0048 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0052 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0086 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LiveVariablesAnalysis.flowThrough(Node,LiveVariableLattice)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LiveVariablesAnalysis.java
Original range: 146-165
Documentation:

Method code:
146 |   @Override
147 |   LiveVariableLattice flowThrough(Node node, LiveVariableLattice input) {
148 |     final BitSet gen = new BitSet(input.liveSet.size());
149 |     final BitSet kill = new BitSet(input.liveSet.size());
150 | 
151 |     // Make kills conditional if the node can end abruptly by an exception.
152 |     boolean conditional = false;
153 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> edgeList = getCfg().getOutEdges(node);
154 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> edge : edgeList) {
155 |       if (Branch.ON_EX.equals(edge.getValue())) {
156 |         conditional = true;
157 |       }
158 |     }
159 |     computeGenKill(node, gen, kill, conditional);
160 |     LiveVariableLattice result = new LiveVariableLattice(input);
161 |     // L_in = L_out - Kill + Gen
162 |     result.liveSet.andNot(kill);
163 |     result.liveSet.or(gen);
164 |     return result;
165 |   }

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 900-921
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
900 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
901 |     switch (n.getType()) {
902 |       case Token.CALL:
903 |       case Token.GETPROP:
904 |       case Token.GETELEM:
905 |       case Token.THROW:
906 |       case Token.NEW:
907 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
908 |       case Token.INC:
909 |       case Token.DEC:
910 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
911 |         return true;
912 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
913 |         return false;
914 |     }
915 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
916 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
917 |         return true;
918 |       }
919 |     }
920 |     return false;
921 |   }

===== M0094 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 228-291
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
228 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
229 |     this.options = options;
230 |     if (errorManager == null) {
231 |       if (outStream == null) {
232 |         setErrorManager(
233 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
234 |       } else {
235 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
236 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
237 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
238 |         setErrorManager(printer);
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
243 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
244 |       options.checkTypes = true;
245 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
246 |       options.checkTypes = false;
247 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
248 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
249 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
250 |       // parser type warnings.
251 |       options.setWarningLevel(
252 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
253 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
254 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
258 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
259 |       options.setWarningLevel(
260 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
261 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
262 |     }
263 | 
264 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
265 |       options.setWarningLevel(
266 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
267 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
268 |     }
269 | 
270 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
271 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
272 |     guards.add(
273 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
274 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
275 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
276 | 
277 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
278 | 
279 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
280 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
281 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
282 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
283 |     // error off.
284 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
285 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
286 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
287 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
288 |     }
289 | 
290 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
291 |   }

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0133 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 486-506
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
486 |   @Override
487 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
488 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
489 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
490 |     if (unknown) {
491 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
492 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
493 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
494 |         unknown = false;
495 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
496 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
497 |             unknown = true;
498 |             break;
499 |           }
500 |         }
501 |       } else {
502 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
503 |       }
504 |     }
505 |     return unknown;
506 |   }

===== M0142 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1988-1990
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1988 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1989 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1990 |   }

===== M0147 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 486-521
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
486 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
487 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
488 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
489 | 
490 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
491 | 
492 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
493 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
494 | 
495 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
496 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
497 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
498 |     }
499 | 
500 |     curNode = n;
501 |     pushScope(n);
502 | 
503 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
504 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
505 |       // scope.
506 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
510 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
511 | 
512 |     // Args
513 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
514 | 
515 |     // Body
516 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
517 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
518 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
519 | 
520 |     popScope();
521 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.DataFlowAnalysis(ControlFlowGraph<N>,JoinOp<L>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 120-130
Documentation:
/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.
   *
   * @see #analyze()
   */
Method code:
120 |   DataFlowAnalysis(ControlFlowGraph<N> targetCfg, JoinOp<L> joinOp) {
121 |     this.cfg = targetCfg;
122 |     this.joinOp = joinOp;
123 |     Comparator<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nodeComparator =
124 |       cfg.getOptionalNodeComparator(isForward());
125 |     if (nodeComparator != null) {
126 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newTreeSet(nodeComparator);
127 |     } else {
128 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
129 |     }
130 |   }

===== M0202 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 294-355
Documentation:

Method code:
294 |   @Override
295 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
296 |     switch (n.getType()) {
297 |       case Token.IF:
298 |         handleIf(n);
299 |         return;
300 |       case Token.WHILE:
301 |         handleWhile(n);
302 |         return;
303 |       case Token.DO:
304 |         handleDo(n);
305 |         return;
306 |       case Token.FOR:
307 |         handleFor(n);
308 |         return;
309 |       case Token.SWITCH:
310 |         handleSwitch(n);
311 |         return;
312 |       case Token.CASE:
313 |         handleCase(n);
314 |         return;
315 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
316 |         handleDefault(n);
317 |         return;
318 |       case Token.BLOCK:
319 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
320 |         handleStmtList(n);
321 |         return;
322 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
323 |         handleFunction(n);
324 |         return;
325 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
326 |         handleExpr(n);
327 |         return;
328 |       case Token.THROW:
329 |         handleThrow(n);
330 |         return;
331 |       case Token.TRY:
332 |         handleTry(n);
333 |         return;
334 |       case Token.CATCH:
335 |         handleCatch(n);
336 |         return;
337 |       case Token.BREAK:
338 |         handleBreak(n);
339 |         return;
340 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
341 |         handleContinue(n);
342 |         return;
343 |       case Token.RETURN:
344 |         handleReturn(n);
345 |         return;
346 |       case Token.WITH:
347 |         handleWith(n);
348 |         return;
349 |       case Token.LABEL:
350 |         return;
351 |       default:
352 |         handleStmt(n);
353 |         return;
354 |     }
355 |   }

===== M0204 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2923-2938
Documentation:
/**
   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.
   */
Method code:
2923 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2924 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
2925 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2926 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2927 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2928 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2929 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
2930 |         // on ASSIGNs
2931 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2932 |       } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
2933 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2934 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2935 |       }
2936 |     }
2937 |     return fnInfo;
2938 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
877 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
878 |     if (item == null) {
879 |       return null;
880 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
881 |       return item.getNext();
882 |     } else {
883 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
884 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
885 |         return item.chain(result);
886 |       } else {
887 |         return item;
888 |       }
889 |     }
890 |   }

===== M0248 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 304-336
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
304 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
305 |     if (prototype == null) {
306 |       return false;
307 |     }
308 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
309 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
310 |       return false;
311 |     }
312 | 
313 |     boolean replacedPrototype = prototype != null;
314 |     this.prototype = prototype;
315 | 
316 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
317 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
318 |       if (superClass != null) {
319 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
320 |       }
321 | 
322 |       if (isInterface()) {
323 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
324 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
325 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
326 |           }
327 |         }
328 |       }
329 |     }
330 | 
331 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
332 |       clearCachedValues();
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     return true;
336 |   }

===== M0254 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 99-160
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
99 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
100 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
101 |     // just bail out early here.
102 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
103 |       return this;
104 |     }
105 | 
106 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
107 | 
108 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
109 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
110 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
111 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
112 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
113 |     }
114 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
115 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
116 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
117 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
118 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
119 |         }
120 |       } else {
121 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
122 |           return this;
123 |         }
124 | 
125 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
126 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
127 |         // one another.
128 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
129 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
130 |           JSType current = it.next();
131 | 
132 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
133 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
134 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
135 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
136 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
137 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
138 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
139 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
140 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
141 |               return this;
142 |             }
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
145 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
146 |               return this;
147 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
148 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
149 |               it.remove();
150 |             }
151 |           }
152 |         }
153 |         alternates.add(alternate);
154 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
155 |       }
156 |     } else {
157 |       result = null;
158 |     }
159 |     return this;
160 |   }

===== M0256 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 286-324
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the new flow state at a given node's entry by merging the
   * output (input) lattice of the node's predecessor (successor).
   *
   * @param node Node to compute new join.
   */
Method code:
286 |   protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
287 |     FlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
288 |     if (isForward()) {
289 |       if (cfg.getEntry() == node) {
290 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
291 |       } else {
292 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> inNodes = cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(node);
293 |         if (inNodes.size() == 1) {
294 |           FlowState<L> inNodeState = inNodes.get(0).getAnnotation();
295 |           state.setIn(inNodeState.getOut());
296 |         } else if (inNodes.size() > 1) {
297 |           List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(inNodes.size());
298 |           for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> currentNode : inNodes) {
299 |             FlowState<L> currentNodeState = currentNode.getAnnotation();
300 |             values.add(currentNodeState.getOut());
301 |           }
302 |           state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |     } else {
306 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> inNodes = cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(node);
307 |       if (inNodes.size() == 1) {
308 |         DiGraphNode<N, Branch> inNode = inNodes.get(0);
309 |         if (inNode == cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
310 |           state.setOut(createEntryLattice());
311 |         } else {
312 |           FlowState<L> inNodeState = inNode.getAnnotation();
313 |           state.setOut(inNodeState.getIn());
314 |         }
315 |       } else if (inNodes.size() > 1) {
316 |         List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(inNodes.size());
317 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> currentNode : inNodes) {
318 |           FlowState<L> currentNodeState = currentNode.getAnnotation();
319 |           values.add(currentNodeState.getIn());
320 |         }
321 |         state.setOut(joinOp.apply(values));
322 |       }
323 |     }
324 |   }

88 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),

===== M0290 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseAtScope(Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 332-353
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a parse tree recursively with a scope, starting at that scope's
   * root.
   */
Method code:
332 |   void traverseAtScope(Scope s) {
333 |     Node n = s.getRootNode();
334 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
335 |       // We need to do some extra magic to make sure that the scope doesn't
336 |       // get re-created when we dive into the function.
337 |       if (inputId == null) {
338 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
339 |       }
340 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
341 |       curNode = n;
342 |       pushScope(s);
343 | 
344 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
345 |       Node body = args.getNext();
346 |       traverseBranch(args, n);
347 |       traverseBranch(body, n);
348 | 
349 |       popScope();
350 |     } else {
351 |       traverseWithScope(n, s);
352 |     }
353 |   }

===== M0292 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 167-188
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
167 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
168 |     if (isAllType) {
169 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
170 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
171 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
172 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
173 |       } else {
174 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
175 |       }
176 |     } else {
177 |       int size = alternates.size();
178 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
179 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
180 |       } else if (size > 1) {
181 |         return null;
182 |       } else if (size == 1) {
183 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
184 |       } else {
185 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
186 |       }
187 |     }
188 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1183 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1184 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1185 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1186 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1187 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1188 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1189 |       } else {
1190 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1191 |       }
1192 |     }
1193 |     return builder.build();
1194 |   }

===== M0299 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.computeEscaped(Scope,Set<Var>,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 548-578
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute set of escaped variables. When a variable is escaped in a
   * dataflow analysis, it can be reference outside of the code that we are
   * analyzing. A variable is escaped if any of the following is true:
   *
   * <p><ol>
   * <li>It is defined as the exception name in CATCH clause so it became a
   * variable local not to our definition of scope.</li>
   * <li>Exported variables as they can be needed after the script terminates.
   * </li>
   * <li>Names of named functions because in javascript, <i>function foo(){}</i>
   * does not kill <i>foo</i> in the dataflow.</li>
   */
Method code:
548 |   static void computeEscaped(final Scope jsScope, final Set<Var> escaped,
549 |       AbstractCompiler compiler) {
550 |     // TODO(user): Very good place to store this information somewhere.
551 |     AbstractPostOrderCallback finder = new AbstractPostOrderCallback() {
552 |       @Override
553 |       public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
554 |         if (jsScope == t.getScope() || !NodeUtil.isName(n)
555 |             || NodeUtil.isFunction(parent)) {
556 |           return;
557 |         }
558 |         String name = n.getString();
559 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name);
560 |         if (var != null && var.scope == jsScope) {
561 |           escaped.add(jsScope.getVar(name));
562 |         }
563 |       }
564 |     };
565 | 
566 |     NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, finder);
567 |     t.traverseAtScope(jsScope);
568 | 
569 |     // 1: Remove the exception name in CATCH which technically isn't local to
570 |     //    begin with.
571 |     for (Iterator<Var> i = jsScope.getVars(); i.hasNext();) {
572 |       Var var = i.next();
573 |       if (var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.CATCH ||
574 |           compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(var.getName())) {
575 |         escaped.add(var);
576 |       }
577 |     }
578 |   }

===== M0300 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 601-659
Documentation:

Method code:
601 |     @Override
602 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
603 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
604 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
605 |       if (name == null) {
606 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
607 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
608 |           errorReporter.error(
609 |             "unnamed function statement",
610 |             sourceName,
611 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
612 |         }
613 |         name = new Name();
614 |         name.setIdentifier("");
615 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
616 |       }
617 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
618 |       Node newName = transform(name);
619 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
620 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
621 |         // declaration.
622 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
623 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
624 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
625 |         // same line as the function keyword.
626 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
627 |             functionNode.getLp();
628 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
629 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
630 |       }
631 | 
632 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
633 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
634 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
635 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
636 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
637 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
638 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
639 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
640 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
641 |       if (fnName != null) {
642 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
643 |       } else {
644 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
645 |       }
646 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
647 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
648 | 
649 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
650 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
651 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
652 |       }
653 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
654 | 
655 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
656 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
657 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
658 |      return node;
659 |     }

===== M0314 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 730-756
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
730 |   @Override
731 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
732 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
733 |       return false;
734 |     }
735 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
736 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
737 |       return false;
738 |     }
739 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
740 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
741 |         return this == that;
742 |       }
743 |       return false;
744 |     }
745 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
747 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
748 |       }
749 |       return false;
750 |     }
751 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
752 |       return false;
753 |     }
754 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
755 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
756 |   }

===== M0353 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0354 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 832-874
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
832 |   @Override
833 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
834 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
835 |       return true;
836 |     }
837 | 
838 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
839 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
840 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
841 |         return true;
842 |       }
843 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
844 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
845 |         return false;
846 |       }
847 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
848 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
849 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
850 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
851 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
852 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
853 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
854 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
855 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
856 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
857 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
858 | 
859 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
860 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
861 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
862 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
863 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
864 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
865 | 
866 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
867 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
868 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
869 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
870 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
871 |     }
872 | 
873 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
874 |   }

701 |     Node processLabeledStatement(LabeledStatement statementNode) {
702 |       Node node = newNode(Token.LABEL);

704 |       Node cur = node;
705 |       for (Label label : statementNode.getLabels()) {

722 |       return node;

===== M0360 =====

Method code:
304 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
305 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
306 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
307 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
308 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
309 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
310 |       maybeSetLengthFrom(irNode, node);
311 |     } else {
312 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
313 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
314 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
315 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
316 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();

318 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
319 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
320 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(irNode, node);
321 |       }
322 |     }

===== M0372 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LiveVariablesAnalysis.computeGenKill(Node,BitSet,BitSet,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LiveVariablesAnalysis.java
Original range: 178-263
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the GEN and KILL set.
   *
   * @param n Root node.
   * @param gen Local variables that are live because of the instruction at
   *        {@code n} will be added to this set.
   * @param kill Local variables that are killed because of the instruction at
   *        {@code n} will be added to this set.
   * @param conditional {@code true} if any assignments encountered are
   *        conditionally executed. These assignments might not kill a variable.
   */
Method code:
178 |   private void computeGenKill(Node n, BitSet gen, BitSet kill,
179 |       boolean conditional) {
180 | 
181 |     switch (n.getType()) {
182 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
183 |       case Token.BLOCK:
184 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
185 |         return;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.WHILE:
188 |       case Token.DO:
189 |       case Token.IF:
190 |         computeGenKill(NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), gen, kill,
191 |             conditional);
192 |         return;
193 | 
194 |       case Token.FOR:
195 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
196 |           computeGenKill(NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), gen, kill,
197 |               conditional);
198 |         } else {
199 |           // for(x in y) {...}
200 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
201 |           Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
202 |           if (NodeUtil.isVar(lhs)) {
203 |             // for(var x in y) {...}
204 |             lhs = lhs.getLastChild();
205 |           }
206 |             addToSetIfLocal(lhs, kill);
207 |             addToSetIfLocal(lhs, gen);
208 |           computeGenKill(rhs, gen, kill, conditional);
209 |         }
210 |         return;
211 | 
212 |       case Token.VAR:
213 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
214 |           if (c.hasChildren()) {
215 |             computeGenKill(c.getFirstChild(), gen, kill, conditional);
216 |             if (!conditional) {
217 |               addToSetIfLocal(c, kill);
218 |             }
219 |           }
220 |         }
221 |         return;
222 | 
223 |       case Token.AND:
224 |       case Token.OR:
225 |         computeGenKill(n.getFirstChild(), gen, kill, conditional);
226 |         // May short circuit.
227 |         computeGenKill(n.getLastChild(), gen, kill, true);
228 |         return;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.HOOK:
231 |         computeGenKill(n.getFirstChild(), gen, kill, conditional);
232 |         // Assume both sides are conditional.
233 |         computeGenKill(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), gen, kill, true);
234 |         computeGenKill(n.getLastChild(), gen, kill, true);
235 |         return;
236 | 
237 |       case Token.NAME:
238 |         if (isArgumentsName(n)) {
239 |           markAllParametersEscaped();
240 |         } else {
241 |           addToSetIfLocal(n, gen);
242 |         }
243 |         return;
244 | 
245 |       default:
246 |         if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isName(n.getFirstChild())) {
247 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
248 |           if (!conditional) {
249 |             addToSetIfLocal(lhs, kill);
250 |           }
251 |           if (!NodeUtil.isAssign(n)) {
252 |             // assignments such as a += 1 reads a.
253 |             addToSetIfLocal(lhs, gen);
254 |           }
255 |           computeGenKill(lhs.getNext(), gen, kill, conditional);
256 |         } else {
257 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
258 |             computeGenKill(c, gen, kill, conditional);
259 |           }
260 |         }
261 |         return;
262 |     }
263 |   }

===== M0373 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 571-585
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
571 |   public Scope getScope() {
572 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
573 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
574 |       return scope;
575 |     }
576 | 
577 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
578 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
579 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
580 |       scopes.push(scope);
581 |     }
582 |     scopeRoots.clear();
583 | 
584 |     return scope;
585 |   }

===== M0384 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 820-847
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
820 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
821 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
822 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
823 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
824 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
825 |           return;
826 |         }
827 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
828 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
829 | 
830 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
831 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
832 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
835 |           }
836 |         } else { // Has a catch.
837 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
838 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
839 |             return;
840 |           } else {
841 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
842 |           }
843 |         }
844 |         lastJump = handler;
845 |       }
846 |     }
847 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.

===== M0397 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 682-779
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
682 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
683 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
684 |     /*
685 |      * This is the case where:
686 |      *
687 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
688 |      * the script.
689 |      *
690 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
691 |      * the caller of the function.
692 |      *
693 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
694 |      * back to the caller of the function.
695 |      *
696 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
697 |      * asked to traverse.
698 |      *
699 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
700 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
701 |      */
702 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
703 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
704 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
705 |       return null;
706 |     }
707 | 
708 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
709 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
710 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
711 |       case Token.IF:
712 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
713 |       case Token.CASE:
714 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
715 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
716 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
717 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
718 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
719 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
720 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
721 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
722 |           } else {
723 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
724 |           }
725 |         } else {
726 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
727 |         }
728 |         break;
729 |       case Token.FOR:
730 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
731 |           return parent;
732 |         } else {
733 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
734 |         }
735 |       case Token.WHILE:
736 |       case Token.DO:
737 |         return parent;
738 |       case Token.TRY:
739 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
740 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
743 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // CATCH block.
747 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
748 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
749 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
750 |           } else {
751 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
752 |           }
753 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
754 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
755 |           if (cfa != null) {
756 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
757 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
758 |             }
759 |           }
760 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
761 |         }
762 |     }
763 | 
764 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
765 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
766 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
767 | 
768 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
769 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
770 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
774 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
775 |     } else {
776 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
777 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
778 |     }
779 |   }

===== M0404 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionType.UnionType(JSTypeRegistry,Collection<JSType>)
