Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
117 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
118 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
119 | 
120 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
121 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
123 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
127 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
128 |     }
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0441 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 435-483
Documentation:

Method code:
435 |   @Override
436 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
437 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
438 |       return true;
439 |     }
440 | 
441 |     // Union types
442 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
443 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
444 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
445 |       return false;
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     // record types
449 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
450 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
451 |     }
452 | 
453 |     // Interfaces
454 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
455 |     // to the interface instance.
456 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
457 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
458 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
459 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
460 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
461 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
462 |           return true;
463 |         }
464 |       }
465 |     }
466 | 
467 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     // other prototype based objects
476 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
477 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
478 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
479 |       // to avoid guessing.
480 |       return true;
481 |     }
482 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
483 |   }

===== M0443 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 616-628
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
616 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
617 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
618 |     if (typeSet == null) {
619 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
620 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
621 |     }
622 | 
623 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
624 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
625 | 
626 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
627 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
628 |   }

===== M0446 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

===== M0450 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0451 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.LinkedDirectedGraph.getDirectedSuccNodes(DiGraphNode<N, E>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/LinkedDirectedGraph.java
Original range: 240-251
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   @Override
241 |   public List<DiGraphNode<N, E>> getDirectedSuccNodes(
242 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode) {
243 |     if (dNode == null) {
244 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(dNode + " is null");
245 |     }
246 |     List<DiGraphNode<N, E>> nodeList = Lists.newArrayList();
247 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> edge : dNode.getOutEdges()) {
248 |       nodeList.add(edge.getDestination());
249 |     }
250 |     return nodeList;
251 |   }

===== M0453 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1332-1340
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1332 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1333 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1334 |       return -1;
1335 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1336 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1337 |     } else {
1338 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1339 |     }
1340 |   }

1248 |         return Token.BLOCK;

1250 |         return Token.LABEL;

1252 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.EXPR_RESULT:
1253 |         return Token.EXPR_RESULT;
1254 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.SCRIPT:
1255 |         return Token.SCRIPT;
1256 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.GET:
1257 |         return Token.GET;
1258 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.SET:
1259 |         return Token.SET;
1260 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.CONST:
1261 |         return Token.CONST;
1262 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.DEBUGGER:
1263 |         return Token.DEBUGGER;
1264 |     }
1265 | 
1266 |     // Token without name

===== M0474 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

/**
   * Creates a Scope given the parent Scope and the root node of the scope.
   * @param parent  The parent Scope. Cannot be null.
   * @param rootNode  Typically the FUNCTION node.
   */
Method code:
375 |   Scope(Scope parent, Node rootNode) {
376 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(parent);
377 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(rootNode != parent.rootNode);
378 | 
379 |     this.parent = parent;
380 |     this.rootNode = rootNode;
381 |     JSType nodeType = rootNode.getJSType();
382 |     if (nodeType != null && nodeType instanceof FunctionType) {
383 |       thisType = ((FunctionType) nodeType).getTypeOfThis();
384 |     } else {
385 |       thisType = parent.thisType;
386 |     }
387 |     this.isBottom = false;
388 |     this.depth = parent.depth + 1;
389 |   }

===== M0485 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 786-800
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
786 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
787 |     switch (n.getType()) {
788 |       case Token.DO:
789 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
790 |       case Token.FOR:
791 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
792 |           return n;
793 |         }
794 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
795 |       case Token.LABEL:
796 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
797 |       default:
798 |         return n;
799 |     }
800 |   }
